## DEFINISI TRIGGER

----- delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_izin -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_izin on t_pegawai_izin
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_izin WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_pegawai_izin'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_izin WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_pegawai_izin'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur_detail -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur_detail on t_pegawai_lembur_detail
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_pegawai_lembur_detail(no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' ,dbo.getDivisionID(), 'val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_lembur_detail__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_pegawai_lembur_detail(no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' ,dbo.getDivisionID(), 'val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_lembur_detail__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
end


----- update_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur_detail -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur_detail on t_pegawai_lembur_detail
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pegawai_lembur_detail set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_lembur_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pegawai_lembur_detail set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_lembur_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end


----- delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur_detail -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur_detail on t_pegawai_lembur_detail
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_lembur_detail WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai), 't_pegawai_lembur_detail'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_lembur_detail WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai) ,'t_pegawai_lembur_detail'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_t_pemakaian_barang -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_t_pemakaian_barang on t_pemakaian_barang
AFTER INSERT
as
begin

----- update_temp_m_t_pemakaian_barang -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_pemakaian_barang on t_pemakaian_barang
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pemakaian_barang set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pemakaian_barang__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pemakaian_barang set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

begin

----- update_temp_m_t_penambahan_kas -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_penambahan_kas on t_penambahan_kas
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penambahan_kas set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penambahan_kas__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penambahan_kas set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penambahan_kas__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end

AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_penjualan_jasa WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_penjualan_jasa'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_penjualan_jasa WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_penjualan_jasa'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

----- update_temp_m_t_penjualan_jasa_detail -----

AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_jasa_detail set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''',kd_jasa='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jasa)+''',qty='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.qty)+''',harga='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga)+''',diskon='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''' AND kd_jasa='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jasa)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+';key__kd_jasa__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jasa)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__kd_jasa__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jasa)+';val__qty__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.qty)+';val__harga__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga)+';val__diskon__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_jasa_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,kd_jasa,qty,harga,diskon FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,kd_jasa,qty,harga,diskon FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- update_temp_m_t_penjualan_nota_kosong -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_penjualan_nota_kosong on t_penjualan_nota_kosong
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_nota_kosong set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_nota_kosong__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_nota_kosong set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_nota_kosong__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

end

----- update_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift -----

AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pegawai_ganti_shift set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_ganti_shift__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_ganti_shift__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end

as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_selected set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',total='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.total)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__total__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.total)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_selected__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,total FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,total FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_selected set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',total='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.total)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__total__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.total)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_selected__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,total FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,total FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift on t_pegawai_ganti_shift
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_ganti_shift WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_pegawai_ganti_shift'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_ganti_shift WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_pegawai_ganti_shift'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- update_temp_m_t_penjualan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_penjualan on t_penjualan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = no_transaksi
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = no_transaksi
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%t_penjualan%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT ('UPDATE t_penjualan SET '
    +(SELECT ('no_transaksi='''+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+''',divisi_id= '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',kd_customer= '''+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',kd_divisi='''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''',kd_jenis='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis)+''',kd_kas='''+convert(varchar,kd_kas)+''',kd_voucher='''+convert(varchar,kd_voucher)+''',no_bukti='''+convert(varchar,no_bukti)+''',tanggal='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal),108)+''',tanggal_jatuh_tempo='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_jatuh_tempo),108)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+''',diskon1='''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''',diskon2='''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''',diskon3='''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''',diskon4='''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''',diskon_uang='''+convert(varchar,diskon_uang)+''',pajak='''+convert(varchar,pajak)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+convert(varchar,kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_server),108)+''',tanggal_setor='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_setor),108)+'''') FROM inserted) 
    +(SELECT ' WHERE no_transaksi='''+no_transaksi+''';' FROM deleted))
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar,no_transaksi) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar,kd_kas)+';val__kd_voucher__'+convert(varchar,kd_voucher)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar,no_bukti)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal),108)+';val__tanggal_jatuh_tempo__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_jatuh_tempo),108)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__diskon_uang__'+convert(varchar,diskon_uang)+';val__pajak__'+convert(varchar,pajak)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar,kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_server),108)+';val__tanggal_setor__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_setor),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
't_penjualan__update'
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT ('UPDATE t_penjualan SET '

----- delete_temp_m_t_penjualan_selected -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_penjualan_selected on t_penjualan_selected
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_penjualan_selected WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_penjualan_selected'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_penjualan_selected WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_penjualan_selected'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

----- update_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail on t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''',kd_shift='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_shift)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__kd_shift__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_shift)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,kd_shift FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,kd_shift FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''',kd_shift='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_shift)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__kd_shift__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_shift)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,kd_shift FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,kd_pegawai,kd_shift FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end

----- delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail on t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai), 't_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai) ,'t_pegawai_ganti_shift_detail'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_prive on t_prive
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_prive set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_prive__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_prive set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_prive__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas,nominal,keterangan,kd_user FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end

----- insert_temp_m_t_penjualan_detail -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_t_penjualan_detail on t_penjualan_detail
AFTER INSERT
as
begin

DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40),@harga_beli_terakhir float,@kd_barang varchar(40),@kd_satuan varchar(40),@kd_customer varchar(10)
SELECT @primary = no_transaksi,@kd_barang=kd_barang, @kd_satuan=kd_satuan
FROM inserted
set @kd_customer=(SELECT kd_customer FROM t_penjualan WHERE no_transaksi=@primary)
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%t_penjualan_detail%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if(@kd_customer='CAA006')
begin
    set @harga_beli_terakhir=(SELECT TOP 1 harga_beli FROM v_get_last_purchase_per_item WHERE kd_barang=@kd_barang AND kd_satuan=@kd_satuan);
end
else
begin
    set @harga_beli_terakhir=(SELECT TOP 1 harga_beli FROM v_get_last_purchase_per_item WHERE kd_barang=@kd_barang AND kd_satuan=@kd_satuan);
end
if (@harga_beli_terakhir is null)
begin
SELECT @harga_beli_terakhir = 0
end
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_penjualan_detail (no_transaksi,kd_barang,kd_satuan,kd_pegawai,jenis,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,harga_jual,qty,point1,point2,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_pegawai)+''','''+convert(varchar,jenis)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''','''+convert(varchar,CONVERT(VARCHAR,@harga_beli_terakhir))+''','''+convert(varchar,qty)+''','''+convert(varchar,point1)+''','''+convert(varchar,point2)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__no_transaksi__'+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar,kd_pegawai)+';val__jenis__'+convert(varchar,jenis)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar,CONVERT(VARCHAR,@harga_beli_terakhir))+';val__qty__'+convert(varchar,qty)+';val__point1__'+convert(varchar,point1)+';val__point2__'+convert(varchar,point2)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
't_penjualan_detail__insert'
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_penjualan_detail (no_transaksi,kd_barang,kd_satuan,kd_pegawai,jenis,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,harga_jual,qty,point1,point2,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_pegawai)+''','''+convert(varchar,jenis)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''','''+convert(varchar,CONVERT(VARCHAR,@harga_beli_terakhir))+''','''+convert(varchar,qty)+''','''+convert(varchar,point1)+''','''+convert(varchar,point2)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__no_transaksi__'+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar,kd_pegawai)+';val__jenis__'+convert(varchar,jenis)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar,CONVERT(VARCHAR,@harga_beli_terakhir))+';val__qty__'+convert(varchar,qty)+';val__point1__'+convert(varchar,point1)+';val__point2__'+convert(varchar,point2)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
't_penjualan_detail__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end



----- update_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_pegawai_lembur on t_pegawai_lembur
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_pegawai_lembur set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''',awal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.awal)+''',akhir='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.akhir)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__awal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.awal)+';val__akhir__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.akhir)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' AFTER UPDATE
as
begin

end

AFTER INSERT
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_transaksi_barang(no_transaksi,kd_divisi,tanggal,keterangan,no_nota,penerima,gudang,kd_user,jenis,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''','''+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.penerima)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gudang)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jenis)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' ,dbo.getDivisionID(), 'val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__no_nota__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+';val__penerima__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.penerima)+';val__gudang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gudang)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jenis)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_transaksi_barang__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_transaksi_barang(no_transaksi,kd_divisi,tanggal,keterangan,no_nota,penerima,gudang,kd_user,jenis,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''','''+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.penerima)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gudang)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jenis)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' ,dbo.getDivisionID(), 'val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__no_nota__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+';val__penerima__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.penerima)+';val__gudang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gudang)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jenis)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_transaksi_barang__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
end

';val__no_order__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_order)+';val__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_customer)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__kd_voucher__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_voucher)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_terima),108)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon4)+';val__diskon_uang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon_uang)+';val__pajak__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.pajak)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__jaminan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jaminan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' 't_penjualan_order__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_order,kd_customer,kd_divisi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,kd_voucher,no_bukti,tanggal,tanggal_terima,status,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,diskon_uang,pajak,keterangan,jaminan,kd_user,tanggal_server,no_transaksi FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_t_penjualan_order -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_penjualan_order on t_penjualan_order
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_penjualan_order WHERE no_order='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_order__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order), 't_penjualan_order'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_penjualan_order WHERE no_order='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_order__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order) ,'t_penjualan_order'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- delete_temp_m_t_transaksi_barang -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_transaksi_barang on t_transaksi_barang
AFTER DELETE
as

AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_retur_detail set no_retur='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_retur)+''',kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+''',kd_satuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_satuan)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''',harga_jual='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), ';val__no_retur__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_retur)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_satuan)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_jual)+';val__qty__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.qty)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_retur_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_retur,kd_barang,kd_satuan,kd_pegawai,harga_jual,qty,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4 FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_retur,kd_barang,kd_satuan,kd_pegawai,harga_jual,qty,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4 FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_penjualan_retur_detail set no_retur='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_retur)+''',kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_jual)+''',qty='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.qty)+''',diskon1='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon1)+''',diskon2='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon2)+''',diskon3='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon3)+''',diskon4='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon4)+'''' +' WHERE no_retur='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_retur__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+ ';val__no_retur__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_retur)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_satuan)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_jual)+';val__qty__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.qty)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_penjualan_retur_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_retur,kd_barang,kd_satuan,kd_pegawai,harga_jual,qty,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4 FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_retur,kd_barang,kd_satuan,kd_pegawai,harga_jual,qty,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4 FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- trig_update_stok_barang_awal -----
CREATE    TRIGGER trig_update_stok_barang_awal
ON m_barang_divisi
FOR DELETE, INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER

/*
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM inserted)
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi = inserted.kd_divisi, 
			@barang = inserted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = inserted.stok_awal,
			@satuan = dbo.GetSatuanTerkecil(kd_barang)
		FROM inserted 
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END

	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)	
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi = deleted.kd_divisi, 
			@barang = deleted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = deleted.stok_awal * -1,
			@satuan = dbo.GetSatuanTerkecil(kd_barang)
		FROM deleted 
	
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan	
	END
*/
END

----- update_temp_m_m_barang_gambar -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_barang_gambar on m_barang_gambar
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_gambar set kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+''',nomor='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nomor)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',gambar='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gambar)+''',ismain='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.ismain)+''',spesifikasi='''+ convert(varchar(255),ISNULL(inserted_data.spesifikasi,''))+''',deskripsi='''+ convert(varchar(255),ISNULL(inserted_data.deskripsi,''))+'''' +' WHERE kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND nomor='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.nomor)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+';key__nomor__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.nomor)+ ';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+';val__nomor__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nomor)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__gambar__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gambar)+';val__ismain__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.ismain)+';val__spesifikasi__'+convert(varchar(255),ISNULL(inserted_data.spesifikasi,''))+';val__deskripsi__'+convert(varchar(255),ISNULL(inserted_data.deskripsi,''))+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 'm_barang_gambar__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_barang,nomor,keterangan,gambar,ismain,spesifikasi,deskripsi FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_barang,nomor,keterangan,gambar,ismain,spesifikasi,deskripsi FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- trig_update_stok_pembelian -----
CREATE  TRIGGER trig_update_stok_pembelian
ON t_pembelian
for DELETE
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER


	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		DECLARE CUR_LOOP  CURSOR FOR
			SELECT deleted.kd_divisi, ob.kd_barang, ob.qty, ob.kd_satuan
			FROM deleted INNER JOIN t_pembelian_detail ob ON ob.no_transaksi = deleted.no_transaksi
		OPEN CUR_LOOP 
		FETCH NEXT FROM CUR_LOOP INTO @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
		WHILE (@@FETCH_STATUS = 0)
		BEGIN
			--EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
			FETCH NEXT FROM CUR_LOOP INTO @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan			
		END
	END
END






----- delete_temp_m_m_barang_gambar -----

AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM m_barang_gambar WHERE kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND nomor='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.nomor)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+';key__nomor__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.nomor), 'm_barang_gambar'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM m_barang_gambar WHERE kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND nomor='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.nomor)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+';key__nomor__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.nomor) ,'m_barang_gambar'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

begin

----- trig_insert_penjualan_detail_pegawai -----
CREATE        TRIGGER trig_insert_penjualan_detail_pegawai
ON dbo.t_penjualan
FOR INSERT
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @no_transaksi VARCHAR(20),
		@kode_divisi VARCHAR(6)
		

	SELECT @no_transaksi = i.no_transaksi, @kode_divisi=i.kd_divisi FROM   inserted i 

	--insert tabel t_penjualan_detail_pegawai (tujuan = komisi pegawai)
	EXEC dbo.sp_insert_t_penjualan_detail_pegawai @no_transaksi, @kode_divisi
END


----- delete_temp_m_t_mutasi_stok_detail -----

as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_mutasi_stok_detail WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan), 't_mutasi_stok_detail'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_mutasi_stok_detail WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_satuan) ,'t_mutasi_stok_detail'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- trig_absensi_pegawai_lain -----
CREATE TRIGGER trig_absensi_pegawai_lain ON [dbo].[t_absensi_pegawai_lain] 
FOR INSERT
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @kdPegawai as varchar(20)
	DECLARE @tanggal as datetime
	DECLARE @jenis as integer

	SELECT @kdPegawai = kd_pegawai,
		 @tanggal = tanggal,
		@jenis = jenis FROM inserted

	IF @jenis = 0	-- Kondisi OUT
	BEGIN
		UPDATE t_absensi
			SET keluar = @tanggal
			WHERE (kd_pegawai = @kdPegawai) AND
			(dbo.GetFormatTanggal(masuk) = dbo.getFormatTanggal(@tanggal))
	END
	ELSE			--- Kondisi IN
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO t_absensi (kd_pegawai,masuk) VALUES
			(@kdPegawai, @tanggal)
	END
		
END

----- trig_absensi_pegawai_lain_delete -----
CREATE TRIGGER trig_absensi_pegawai_lain_delete ON [dbo].[t_absensi_pegawai_lain] 
FOR DELETE
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @kdPegawai as varchar(20)
	DECLARE @tanggal as datetime
	DECLARE @jenis as integer

	SELECT @kdPegawai = kd_pegawai,
		 @tanggal = tanggal,
		@jenis = jenis FROM deleted

	IF @jenis = 0	-- Kondisi OUT
	BEGIN
		UPDATE t_absensi
			SET keluar = null
			WHERE (kd_pegawai = @kdPegawai) AND
			(masuk = dbo.GetTanggalMasuk(@kdPegawai,@tanggal,2))
	END
	ELSE			--- Kondisi IN
	BEGIN
		DELETE FROM t_absensi 
		WHERE (kd_pegawai = @kdPegawai) AND
			(masuk = dbo.GetTanggalMasuk(@kdPegawai,@tanggal,2))
	END
		
END

----- update_temp_m_t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan on t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_jenis='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',no_bukti='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_hutang_pegawai_cicilan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_transaksi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,no_bukti,tanggal,nominal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_transaksi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,no_bukti,tanggal,nominal,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',keterangan='''+ CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_hutang_pegawai_cicilan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER ----- delete_temp_m_t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan on t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan), 't_hutang_pegawai_cicilan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan) ,'t_hutang_pegawai_cicilan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

----- delete_temp_m_t_pembelian -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_pembelian on t_pembelian
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pembelian WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_pembelian'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pembelian WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_pembelian'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

----- update_temp_m_t_hutang_cicilan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_hutang_cicilan on t_hutang_cicilan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_jenis='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',no_bukti='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_hutang_cicilan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_transaksi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,nominal,tanggal,no_bukti,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_transaksi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,nominal,tanggal,no_bukti,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',kd_jenis='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',no_bukti='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_hutang_cicilan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_transaksi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,nominal,tanggal,no_bukti,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_terima),108)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon4)+';val__pajak__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.pajak)+';val__ppnbm__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.ppnbm)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__jaminan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jaminan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pembelian_order__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_order,no_pp_order,kd_divisi,kd_supplier,kd_kas,kd_jenis_bayar,tanggal,tanggal_terima,status,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,pajak,ppnbm,no_bukti,keterangan,jaminan,kd_user,tanggal_server,no_transaksi FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_t_pembelian_order -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_pembelian_order on t_pembelian_order
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pembelian_order WHERE no_order='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_order__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order), 't_pembelian_order'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pembelian_order WHERE no_order='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_order__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_order) ,'t_pembelian_order'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.jenis)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), ----- update_temp_m_m_divisi -----
