AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE m_divisi set kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''',nama='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nama)+''',kepala_nota='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kepala_nota)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',status='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+'''' +' WHERE kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+ ';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nama)+';val__kepala_nota__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kepala_nota)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 'm_divisi__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,nama,kepala_nota,keterangan,status FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,nama,kepala_nota,keterangan,status FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE m_divisi set kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''',nama='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nama)+''',kepala_nota='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kepala_nota)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',status='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+'''' +' WHERE kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+ ';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nama)+';val__kepala_nota__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kepala_nota)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 'm_divisi__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,nama,kepala_nota,keterangan,status FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,nama,kepala_nota,keterangan,status FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end

----- update_temp_m_m_barang_divisi -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_barang_divisi on m_barang_divisi
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_divisi set kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''',kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+''',stok_awal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_awal)+''',harga_beli_awal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_beli_awal)+''',stok_min='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_min)+''',status='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+''',point='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.point)+'''' +' WHERE kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+ ';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+';val__stok_awal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_awal)+';val__harga_beli_awal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_beli_awal)+';val__stok_min__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_min)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+';val__point__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.point)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 'm_barang_divisi__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,kd_barang,stok_awal,harga_beli_awal,stok_min,status,point FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,kd_barang,stok_awal,harga_beli_awal,stok_min,status,point FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_divisi set kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+''',kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+''',stok_awal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_awal)+''',harga_beli_awal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_beli_awal)+''',stok_min='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_min)+''',status='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+''',point='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.point)+'''' +' WHERE kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+ ';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_divisi)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+';val__stok_awal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_awal)+';val__harga_beli_awal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga_beli_awal)+';val__stok_min__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.stok_min)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.status)+';val__point__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.point)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 'm_barang_divisi__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,kd_barang,stok_awal,harga_beli_awal,stok_min,status,point FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_divisi,kd_barang,stok_awal,harga_beli_awal,stok_min,status,point FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_t_pembelian_retur -----

AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pembelian_retur WHERE no_retur='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_retur__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur), 't_pembelian_retur'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pembelian_retur WHERE no_retur='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_retur__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_retur) ,'t_pembelian_retur'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- delete_temp_m_m_barang_divisi -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_barang_divisi on m_barang_divisi
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM m_barang_divisi WHERE kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang), 'm_barang_divisi'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM m_barang_divisi WHERE kd_divisi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_divisi)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_barang) ,'m_barang_divisi'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

';val__no_retur__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_retur)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_satuan)+';val__qty__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.qty)+';val__harga__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.harga)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pembelian_retur_detail__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_retur,kd_barang,kd_satuan,qty,harga,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4 FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_retur,kd_barang,kd_satuan,qty,harga,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4 FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_biaya_operasional WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_biaya_operasional'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data

----- trig_update_stok_penjualan_detail -----

CREATE  TRIGGER trig_update_stok_penjualan_detail
ON t_penjualan_detail
FOR  DELETE, INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER


	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM inserted)
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-', 
			@barang = inserted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = inserted.qty* -1,
			@satuan = inserted.kd_satuan
		FROM inserted
		--INNER JOIN t_pembelian ob ON ob.no_transaksi = inserted.no_transaksi
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)	
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-',
			@barang = deleted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = deleted.qty,
			@satuan = deleted.kd_satuan
		FROM deleted-- INNER JOIN t_pembelian ob ON ob.no_transaksi = deleted.no_transaksi
	
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan	
	END

END



----- insert_temp_m_m_customer -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_customer on m_customer
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_customer
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_customer%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_customer(kd_customer,kd_kota,nama,alamat,telepon,fax,kontak,hp,email,point,limit_kredit,disc,status,parent,keterangan,npwp_no,nppkp_no,npwp_nama,npwp_alamat,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_kota )+''','''+convert(varchar,nama )+''','''+convert(varchar,alamat )+''','''+convert(varchar,telepon )+''','''+convert(varchar,fax )+''','''+convert(varchar,kontak )+''','''+convert(varchar,hp )+''','''+convert(varchar,email )+''','''+convert(varchar,point )+''','''+convert(varchar,limit_kredit )+''','''+convert(varchar,disc )+''','''+convert(varchar,status )+''','''+convert(varchar,parent )+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,npwp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,nppkp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,npwp_nama COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,npwp_alamat COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__kd_kota__'+convert(varchar,kd_kota )+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama )+';val__alamat__'+convert(varchar,alamat )+';val__telepon__'+convert(varchar,telepon )+';val__fax__'+convert(varchar,fax)+';val__kontak__'+convert(varchar,kontak )+';val__hp__'+convert(varchar,hp )+';val__email__'+convert(varchar,email )+';val__point__'+convert(varchar,point )+';val__limit_kredit__'+convert(varchar,limit_kredit )+';val__disc__'+convert(varchar,disc )+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status )+';val__parent__'+convert(varchar,parent )+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__npwp_no__'+convert(varchar,npwp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__nppkp_no__'+convert(varchar,nppkp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__npwp_nama__'+convert(varchar,npwp_nama COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__npwp_alamat__'+convert(varchar,npwp_alamat COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_customer__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_customer(kd_customer,kd_kota,nama,alamat,telepon,fax,kontak,hp,email,point,limit_kredit,disc,status,parent,keterangan,npwp_no,nppkp_no,npwp_nama,npwp_alamat,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_kota )+''','''+convert(varchar,nama )+''','''+convert(varchar,alamat )+''','''+convert(varchar,telepon )+''','''+convert(varchar,fax )+''','''+convert(varchar,kontak )+''','''+convert(varchar,hp )+''','''+convert(varchar,email )+''','''+convert(varchar,point )+''','''+convert(varchar,limit_kredit )+''','''+convert(varchar,disc )+''','''+convert(varchar,status )+''','''+convert(varchar,parent )+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,npwp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,nppkp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,npwp_nama COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,npwp_alamat COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),

----- trig_update_stok_pembelian_detail -----


CREATE  TRIGGER trig_update_stok_pembelian_detail
ON t_pembelian_detail
FOR  DELETE, INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER


	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM inserted)
	BEGIN
		--SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-', 
			@barang = inserted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = inserted.qty,
			@satuan = inserted.kd_satuan
		FROM inserted
		--INNER JOIN t_pembelian ob ON ob.no_transaksi = inserted.no_transaksi
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)	
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-',
			@barang = deleted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = deleted.qty * -1,
			@satuan = deleted.kd_satuan
		FROM deleted-- INNER JOIN t_pembelian ob ON ob.no_transaksi = deleted.no_transaksi
	
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan	
	END


END



----- update_temp_m_m_customer -----
CREATE TRIGGER [dbo].[update_temp_m_m_customer] on [dbo].[m_customer]
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_customer
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_customer
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_customer%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_customer set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',kd_kota = '''+convert(varchar,kd_kota)+''',nama='''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',alamat='''+convert(varchar,alamat)+''',telepon='''+convert(varchar,telepon)+''',fax='''+convert(varchar,fax)+''',kontak='''+convert(varchar,kontak)+''',hp='''+convert(varchar,hp)+''',email='''+convert(varchar,email)+''',point='''+convert(varchar,point)+''',limit_kredit='''+convert(varchar,limit_kredit)+''',disc='''+convert(varchar,disc)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+''',parent='''+convert(varchar,parent)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',npwp_no='''+convert(varchar,npwp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',nppkp_no='''+convert(varchar,nppkp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',npwp_nama='''+convert(varchar,npwp_nama COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',npwp_alamat='''+convert(varchar,npwp_alamat COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_customer='''+convert(varchar,kd_customer)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer) FROM deleted)+
(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__alamat__'+convert(varchar,alamat)+';val_telepon__'+convert(varchar,telepon)+';val__fax__'+convert(varchar,fax)+';val__kontak__'+convert(varchar,kontak)+';val__hp__'+convert(varchar,hp)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_customer__update'
INSERT INTO tbl_log(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_customer set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',kd_kota = '''+convert(varchar,kd_kota)+''',nama='''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',alamat='''+convert(varchar,alamat)+''',telepon='''+convert(varchar,telepon)+''',fax='''+convert(varchar,fax)+''',kontak='''+convert(varchar,kontak)+''',hp='''+convert(varchar,hp)+''',email='''+convert(varchar,email)+''',point='''+convert(varchar,point)+''',limit_kredit='''+convert(varchar,limit_kredit)+''',disc='''+convert(varchar,disc)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+''',parent='''+convert(varchar,parent)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',npwp_no='''+convert(varchar,npwp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',nppkp_no='''+convert(varchar,nppkp_no COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',npwp_nama='''+convert(varchar,npwp_nama COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''',npwp_alamat='''+convert(varchar,npwp_alamat COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_customer='''+convert(varchar,kd_customer)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_customer__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

'+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_pendapatan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- trig_update_stok_penjualan_retur_detail -----


CREATE  TRIGGER trig_update_stok_penjualan_retur_detail
ON t_penjualan_retur_detail
FOR  DELETE, INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER


	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM inserted)
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-', 
			@barang = inserted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = inserted.qty,
			@satuan = inserted.kd_satuan
		FROM inserted
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)	
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-',
			@barang = deleted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = deleted.qty * -1,
			@satuan = deleted.kd_satuan
		FROM deleted
	
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan	
	END


END



----- delete_temp_m_m_customer -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_customer on m_customer
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_customer
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_customer%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_customer WHERE kd_customer='''+convert(varchar,kd_customer)+''';') FROM deleted)
    ,dbo.getDivisionID()
    ,(SELECT 'key__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer) FROM deleted),
    'm_customer__delete'
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_customer WHERE kd_customer='''+convert(varchar,kd_customer)+''';') FROM deleted)
    ,dbo.getDivisionID()
    ,(SELECT 'key__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer) FROM deleted),
    'm_customer__delete'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_pendapatan WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_pendapatan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

CREATE  TRIGGER trig_update_stok_pembelian_retur_detail
ON t_pembelian_retur_detail
FOR  DELETE, INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER


	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM inserted)
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-', 
			@barang = inserted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = inserted.qty * -1,
			@satuan = inserted.kd_satuan
		FROM inserted
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)	
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 

		SELECT @divisi ='-',
			@barang = deleted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = deleted.qty,
			@satuan = deleted.kd_satuan
		FROM deleted
	
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan	
	END


END

AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_warna
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_warna%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_warna (kd_warna,nama,status,keterangan,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_warna__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_warna (kd_warna,nama,status,keterangan,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_warna__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

----- trig_update_stok_opname_stok -----

CREATE  TRIGGER trig_update_stok_opname_stok
ON t_opname_stok
FOR  DELETE, INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @divisi JR_KODE_MASTER, @barang JR_KODE_BARANG,
		@jumlah JR_ANGKA_KOMA, @satuan JR_KODE_MASTER, @status JR_STATUS_JENIS


	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM inserted)
	BEGIN
		--SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = '' 
		SET @status=0

		SELECT @divisi ='-', 
			@barang = inserted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = inserted.qty,
			@satuan = inserted.kd_satuan,
			@status=inserted.status
		FROM inserted
		
		if @status <> 2
			SET @jumlah = @jumlah * -1

		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END
	
	IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM deleted)	
	BEGIN
		SET @divisi = ''
		SET @barang = ''
		SET @jumlah = 0
		SET @satuan = ''
		SET @status=0

		SELECT @divisi ='-',
			@barang = deleted.kd_barang,
			@jumlah = deleted.qty * -1,
			@satuan = deleted.kd_satuan,
			@status=deleted.status
		FROM deleted-- INNER JOIN t_pembelian ob ON ob.no_transaksi = deleted.no_transaksi
	
		if @status<>2
			SET @jumlah= @jumlah *-1
			
		EXEC dbo.sp_update_stok_akhir @divisi, @barang, @jumlah, @satuan
	END


END




----- update_temp_m_m_warna -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_warna on m_warna
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_warna
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_warna
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_warna%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_warna SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama) FROM m_warna)+(SELECT ';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_warna__update'


INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_warna SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_warna__update'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- update_temp_m_t_surat_berharga -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_surat_berharga on t_surat_berharga
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_surat_berharga set no_surat_berharga='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_surat_berharga)+''',kd_bank='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_bank)+''',kd_jenis='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+''',kd_pihak_lain='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pihak_lain)+''',nominal='''+ ----- delete_temp_m_m_warna -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_warna on m_warna
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_warna
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_warna%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_warna WHERE kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna) FROM deleted),
'm_warna__delete'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_warna WHERE kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna) FROM deleted),
'm_warna__delete'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- delete_temp_m_t_surat_berharga -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_surat_berharga on t_surat_berharga
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_surat_berharga WHERE no_surat_berharga='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_surat_berharga)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_surat_berharga__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_surat_berharga), 't_surat_berharga'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_surat_berharga WHERE no_surat_berharga='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_surat_berharga)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_surat_berharga__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_surat_berharga) ,'t_surat_berharga'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_m_jenis_bahan -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_jenis_bahan on m_jenis_bahan
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_jenis_bahan
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_jenis_bahan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_jenis_bahan (kd_jenis_bahan,nama,keterangan,status,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_jenis_bahan__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_jenis_bahan (kd_jenis_bahan,nama,keterangan,status,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_jenis_bahan__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

----- update_temp_m_m_jenis_bahan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_jenis_bahan on m_jenis_bahan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_jenis_bahan
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_jenis_bahan
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_jenis_bahan%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_jenis_bahan SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_jenis_bahan__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_jenis_bahan SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_jenis_bahan__update'


end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- update_temp_m_t_mutasi_kas -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_mutasi_kas on t_mutasi_kas
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_mutasi_kas set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',kd_kas_sumber='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_sumber)+''',kd_kas_tujuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_tujuan)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',no_bukti_sumber='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_sumber)+''',no_bukti_tujuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_tujuan)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__kd_kas_sumber__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_sumber)+';val__kd_kas_tujuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_tujuan)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__no_bukti_sumber__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_sumber)+';val__no_bukti_tujuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_tujuan)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_mutasi_kas__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas_sumber,kd_kas_tujuan,nominal,no_bukti_sumber,no_bukti_tujuan,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas_sumber,kd_kas_tujuan,nominal,no_bukti_sumber,no_bukti_tujuan,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_mutasi_kas set no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',kd_kas_sumber='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_sumber)+''',kd_kas_tujuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_tujuan)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',no_bukti_sumber='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_sumber)+''',no_bukti_tujuan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_tujuan)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+ ';val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_transaksi)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__kd_kas_sumber__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_sumber)+';val__kd_kas_tujuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas_tujuan)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__no_bukti_sumber__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_sumber)+';val__no_bukti_tujuan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti_tujuan)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_mutasi_kas__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas_sumber,kd_kas_tujuan,nominal,no_bukti_sumber,no_bukti_tujuan,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_transaksi,tanggal,kd_kas_sumber,kd_kas_tujuan,nominal,no_bukti_sumber,no_bukti_tujuan,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end


----- delete_temp_m_m_jenis_bahan -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_jenis_bahan on m_jenis_bahan
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_jenis_bahan
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_jenis_bahan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_jenis_bahan WHERE kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan) FROM deleted),
'm_jenis_bahan__delete'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_jenis_bahan WHERE kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan) FROM deleted),
'm_jenis_bahan__delete'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- delete_temp_m_t_mutasi_kas -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_mutasi_kas on t_mutasi_kas
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_mutasi_kas WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_mutasi_kas'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_mutasi_kas WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_mutasi_kas'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_m_kategori -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_kategori on m_kategori
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_kategori
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_kategori%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_kategori (kd_kategori,nama,keterangan,status,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_kategori__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_kategori (kd_kategori,nama,keterangan,status,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_kategori__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

----- update_temp_m_m_kategori -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_kategori on m_kategori
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_kategori
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_kategori
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_kategori%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_kategori SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_kategori='''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_kategori__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_kategori SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_kategori='''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_kategori__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- insert_temp_m_m_supplier -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_supplier on m_supplier
AFTER INSERT
as

AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_aset_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',kd_aset='''+ CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__kd_aset__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_aset)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_m_kategori -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_kategori on m_kategori
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_kategori
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_kategori%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_kategori WHERE kd_kategori='''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori) FROM deleted),
'm_kategori__delete'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_kategori WHERE kd_kategori='''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori) FROM deleted),
'm_kategori__delete'


end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

----- insert_temp_m_m_merk -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_merk on m_merk
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_merk
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_merk%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_merk (kd_merk,nama,status,keterangan,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_merk__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_merk (kd_merk,nama,status,keterangan,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_merk__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_absensi(id,kd_pegawai,masuk,keluar,tanggal_server,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.id)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.masuk)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keluar)+''','''+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' ,dbo.getDivisionID(), 'val__id__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.id)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__masuk__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.masuk)+';val__keluar__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keluar)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_absensi__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data

----- update_temp_m_m_merk -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_merk on m_merk
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_merk
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_merk
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_merk%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_merk SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_merk__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_merk SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_merk__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

----- update_temp_m_t_absensi -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_absensi on t_absensi
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_absensi set id='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.id)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''',masuk='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.masuk)+''',keluar='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keluar)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE id='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.id)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__id__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.id)+ ';val__id__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.id)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__masuk__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.masuk)+';val__keluar__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keluar)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_absensi__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,id,kd_pegawai,masuk,keluar,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,id,kd_pegawai,masuk,keluar,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_absensi set id='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.id)+''',kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+''',masuk='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.masuk)+''',keluar='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keluar)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE id='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.id)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__id__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.id)+ ';val__id__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.id)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__masuk__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.masuk)+';val__keluar__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keluar)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_absensi__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,id,kd_pegawai,masuk,keluar,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,id,kd_pegawai,masuk,keluar,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
end


----- delete_temp_m_m_merk -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_merk on m_merk
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_merk
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_merk%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_merk WHERE kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk) FROM deleted),
'm_merk__delete'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_merk WHERE kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk) FROM deleted),
'm_merk__delete'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' ----- update_temp_m_m_model -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_model on m_model
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_model
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_model
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_model%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_model SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_model='''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_model__'+convert(varchar,kd_model) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_model__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_model SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_model='''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_model__'+convert(varchar,kd_model) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_model__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

'+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jenis)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) ----- delete_temp_m_m_model -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_model on m_model
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_model
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_model%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_model__update WHERE kd_model='''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''';') FROM deleted)
    ,dbo.getDivisionID()
    ,(SELECT 'key__kd_model__'+convert(varchar,kd_model) FROM deleted),
    'm_model__delete'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- delete_temp_m_t_absensi_pegawai_lain -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_absensi_pegawai_lain on t_absensi_pegawai_lain
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_absensi_pegawai_lain WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi), 't_absensi_pegawai_lain'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_absensi_pegawai_lain WHERE no_transaksi='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_transaksi) ,'t_absensi_pegawai_lain'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_m_barang -----

AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40), @data_format VARCHAR(2000), @table_aksi VARCHAR(255)
SELECT @primary = kd_barang
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_barang(kd_barang,kd_kategori,kd_jenis_bahan,kd_model,kd_merk,kd_warna,ukuran,nama,keterangan,status,status_pinjam,pabrik,tanggal_daftar,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''','''+convert(varchar,ukuran)+''','''+nama+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+''','''+convert(varchar,pabrik)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,
dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+';val__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+';val__kd_model__'+convert(varchar,kd_model)+';val__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+';val__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+';val__ukuran__'+convert(varchar,ukuran)+';val__nama__'+nama+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__status_pinjam__'+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+';val__pabrik__'+convert(varchar,pabrik)+';val__tanggal_daftar__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108) FROM inserted),
'm_barang__insert'
-- FROM inserted
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) 
SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_barang(kd_barang,kd_kategori,kd_jenis_bahan,kd_model,kd_merk,kd_warna,ukuran,nama,keterangan,status,status_pinjam,pabrik,tanggal_daftar,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''','''+convert(varchar,ukuran)+''','''+nama+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+''','''+convert(varchar,pabrik)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted),
dbo.getDivisionID(),

VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__periode__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.periode)+';val__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pegawai)+';val__gaji_pokok__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.gaji_pokok)+';val__libur_off_jam__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.libur_off_jam)+';val__libur_off_rp__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.libur_off_rp)+';val__lembur_jam__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lembur_jam)+';val__lembur_rp__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lembur_rp)+';val__kerajinan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kerajinan)+';val__lain_lain_plus__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lain_lain_plus)+';val__pinjaman_lalu__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.pinjaman_lalu)+';val__pinjaman_sekarang__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.pinjaman_sekarang)+';val__absen_jam__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.absen_jam)+';val__absen_rp__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.absen_rp)+';val__jamsostek__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jamsostek)+';val__lain_lain_min__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lain_lain_min)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_gaji__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
end


----- update_temp_m_m_barang -----
CREATE TRIGGER [dbo].[update_temp_m_m_barang] on [dbo].[m_barang]
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_barang
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_barang
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang SET divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',kd_kategori = '''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''',kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''',kd_model='''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''',kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''' ,kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''' ,ukuran='''+convert(varchar,ukuran)+''' ,nama='''+nama+''' ,keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''' ,status='''+convert(varchar,status)+''' ,status_pinjam='''+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+''' ,pabrik='''+convert(varchar,pabrik)+''' ,tanggal_daftar='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108)+'''' FROM inserted) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''';' FROM deleted)
,
dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+';val__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+';val__kd_model__'+convert(varchar,kd_model)+';val__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+';val__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+';val__ukuran__'+convert(varchar,ukuran)+';val__nama__'+nama+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__status_pinjam__'+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+';val__pabrik__'+convert(varchar,pabrik)+';val__tanggal_daftar__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108) FROM inserted),
'm_barang__update'
-- INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id) SELECT('UPDATE m_barang set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',kd_kategori = '''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''',kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''',kd_model='''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''',kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''' ,kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''' ,ukuran='''+convert(varchar,ukuran)+''' ,nama='''+convert(varchar,nama)+''' ,keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''' ,status='''+convert(varchar,status)+''' ,status_pinjam='''+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+''' ,pabrik='''+convert(varchar,pabrik)+''' ,tanggal_daftar='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108)+'''  WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''';'),dbo.getDivisionID() FROM inserted
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',kd_kategori = '''+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+''',kd_jenis_bahan='''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+''',kd_model='''+convert(varchar,kd_model)+''',kd_merk='''+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+''' ,kd_warna='''+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+''' ,ukuran='''+convert(varchar,ukuran)+''' ,nama='''+nama+''' ,keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''' ,status='''+convert(varchar,status)+''' ,status_pinjam='''+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+''' ,pabrik='''+convert(varchar,pabrik)+''' ,tanggal_daftar='''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108)+'''' FROM inserted) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''';' FROM deleted)
,
dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__kd_kategori__'+convert(varchar,kd_kategori)+';val__kd_jenis_bahan__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis_bahan)+';val__kd_model__'+convert(varchar,kd_model)+';val__kd_merk__'+convert(varchar,kd_merk)+';val__kd_warna__'+convert(varchar,kd_warna)+';val__ukuran__'+convert(varchar,ukuran)+';val__nama__'+nama+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__status_pinjam__'+convert(varchar,status_pinjam)+';val__pabrik__'+convert(varchar,pabrik)+';val__tanggal_daftar__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_daftar) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_daftar),108) FROM inserted),
'm_barang__update'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lembur_jam)+''',lembur_rp='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lembur_rp)+''',kerajinan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kerajinan)+''',lain_lain_plus='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lain_lain_plus)+''',pinjaman_lalu='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.pinjaman_lalu)+''',pinjaman_sekarang='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.pinjaman_sekarang)+''',absen_jam='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.absen_jam)+''',absen_rp='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.absen_rp)+''',jamsostek='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.jamsostek)+''',lain_lain_min='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.lain_lain_min)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+'''' +' WHERE no_bayar='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_bayar)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_bayar__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_bayar)+ ';val__no_bayar__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bayar)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + ----- delete_temp_m_m_barang -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_barang on m_barang
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_barang
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_barang WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang) FROM deleted),
'm_barang__delete'
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_barang WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang) FROM deleted),
'm_barang__delete'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- delete_temp_m_t_gaji -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_gaji on t_gaji
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_gaji WHERE no_bayar='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_bayar)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_bayar__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_bayar), 't_gaji'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_gaji WHERE no_bayar='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_bayar)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_bayar__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_bayar) ,'t_gaji'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_m_satuan -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_satuan on m_satuan
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_satuan
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_satuan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_satuan (kd_satuan,nama,keterangan,status,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_satuan__insert'
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_satuan (kd_satuan,nama,keterangan,status,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,nama)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)  FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_satuan__insert'
end
else
begin
select ''
