----- update_temp_m_m_satuan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_satuan on m_satuan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_satuan
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_satuan
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_satuan%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_satuan SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_satuan='''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_satuan__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_satuan SET set divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',nama = '''+convert(varchar,nama)+''',keterangan='''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_satuan='''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan) FROM deleted)+ (SELECT ';val__nama__'+convert(varchar,nama)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_satuan__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- update_temp_m_t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan on t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',no_nota='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',kd_jenis='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',no_bukti='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__no_nota__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_nota,tanggal,nominal,kd_jenis,kd_kas,no_bukti,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_cicilan,no_nota,tanggal,nominal,kd_jenis,kd_kas,no_bukti,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan set no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+''',no_nota='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',nominal='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+''',kd_jenis='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+''',kd_kas='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+''',no_bukti='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+ ';val__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_cicilan)+';val__no_nota__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_nota)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__nominal__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.nominal)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_kas)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_bukti)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_m_satuan -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_satuan on m_satuan
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_satuan
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_satuan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_satuan WHERE kd_satuan='''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan) FROM deleted),
'm_satuan__delete'
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_satuan WHERE kd_satuan='''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan) FROM deleted),
'm_satuan__delete'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end



----- delete_temp_m_t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan on t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan), 't_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan WHERE no_cicilan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_cicilan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_cicilan) ,'t_hutang_biaya_angkut_cicilan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_m_barang_satuan -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_m_barang_satuan on m_barang_satuan
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_barang
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang_satuan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_barang_satuan (kd_barang,kd_satuan,jumlah,harga_jual,status,margin,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,jumlah)+''','''+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,margin)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__jumlah__'+convert(varchar,jumlah)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__margin__'+convert(varchar,margin)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_satuan__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_barang_satuan (kd_barang,kd_satuan,jumlah,harga_jual,status,margin,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,jumlah)+''','''+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,margin)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__jumlah__'+convert(varchar,jumlah)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__margin__'+convert(varchar,margin)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_satuan__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

'+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh(kd_jarak_tempuh,kd_pengisian_bbm,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''','''+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''','''+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' ,dbo.getDivisionID(), 'val__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+';val__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' ----- update_temp_m_m_barang_satuan -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_barang_satuan on m_barang_satuan
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_barang
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_barang
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang_satuan%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_satuan SET margin='''+convert(varchar,margin)+''', divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',jumlah='''+convert(varchar,jumlah)+''',harga_jual='''+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan = '''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__jumlah__'+convert(varchar,jumlah)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__margin__'+convert(varchar,margin)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_satuan__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_satuan SET margin='''+convert(varchar,margin)+''', divisi_id = '''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''',jumlah='''+convert(varchar,jumlah)+''',harga_jual='''+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+''',status='''+convert(varchar,status)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan = '''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__jumlah__'+convert(varchar,jumlah)+';val__harga_jual__'+convert(varchar,harga_jual)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__margin__'+convert(varchar,margin)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_satuan__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh on t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh set kd_jarak_tempuh='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''',kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',KM='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE kd_jarak_tempuh='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+ ';val__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+';val__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__KM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_jarak_tempuh,kd_pengisian_bbm,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_jarak_tempuh,kd_pengisian_bbm,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh set kd_jarak_tempuh='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''',kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE kd_jarak_tempuh='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+ ';val__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+';val__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__KM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_jarak_tempuh,kd_pengisian_bbm,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_jarak_tempuh,kd_pengisian_bbm,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_m_barang_satuan -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_barang_satuan on m_barang_satuan
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_barang
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang_satuan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_barang_satuan WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan = '''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang) FROM deleted),
'm_barang_satuan__delete'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_barang_satuan WHERE kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan = '''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang) FROM deleted),
'm_barang_satuan__delete'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end



----- delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh on t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh WHERE kd_jarak_tempuh='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh), 't_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh WHERE kd_jarak_tempuh='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_jarak_tempuh__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jarak_tempuh) ,'t_kendaraan_jarak_tempuh'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_m_barang_divisi_diskon -----

AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_divisi
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang_divisi_diskon%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_barang_divisi_diskon(kd_divisi,kd_barang,kd_satuan,qty_minim,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__qty_minim__'+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_divisi_diskon__insert'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO m_barang_divisi_diskon(kd_divisi,kd_barang,kd_satuan,qty_minim,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''','''+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';val__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';val__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';val__qty_minim__'+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_divisi_diskon__insert'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

'+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__BBM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.BBM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm__insert' FROM inserted inserted_data
end


----- update_temp_m_m_barang_divisi_diskon -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_m_barang_divisi_diskon on m_barang_divisi_diskon
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary_new varchar(40),@primary_old VARCHAR(40)
SELECT @primary_new = kd_divisi
FROM inserted
SELECT @primary_old = kd_divisi
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang_divisi_diskon%' AND (aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_new+'%' OR aksi LIKE '%'+@primary_old+'%') AND aksi LIKE '%UPDATE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null) AND (@primary_old IS NOT NULL OR @primary_new IS NOT NULL)
begin

INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_divisi_diskon SET diskon1='''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''', diskon2 = '''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''',diskon3='''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''',diskon4='''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_divisi='''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND qty_minim='''+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+''' AND kd_satuan = '''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';key__qty_minim__'+convert(varchar,qty_minim) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_divisi_diskon__update'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'UPDATE m_barang_divisi_diskon SET diskon1='''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''', diskon2 = '''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''',diskon3='''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''',diskon4='''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+'''' FROM inserted ) 
+(SELECT ' WHERE kd_divisi='''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang='''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND qty_minim='''+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+''' AND kd_satuan = '''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''';' FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';key__qty_minim__'+convert(varchar,qty_minim) FROM deleted)+(SELECT ';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
'm_barang_divisi_diskon__update'

end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- update_temp_m_t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm -----
CREATE TRIGGER update_temp_m_t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm on t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm
AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm set kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''',no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',BBM='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.BBM)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+ ';val__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+';val__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__BBM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.BBM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_pengisian_bbm,no_STNK,tanggal,BBM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_pengisian_bbm,no_STNK,tanggal,BBM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm set kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''',no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.BBM)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+ ';val__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+';val__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__BBM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.BBM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,kd_pengisian_bbm,no_STNK,tanggal,BBM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,kd_pengisian_bbm,no_STNK,tanggal,BBM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no

----- delete_temp_m_m_barang_divisi_diskon -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_m_barang_divisi_diskon on m_barang_divisi_diskon
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = kd_divisi
FROM deleted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%m_barang_divisi_diskon%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%DELETE%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_barang_divisi_diskon WHERE kd_divisi='''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang = '''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan='''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''' AND qty_minim='''+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';key__qty_minim__'+convert(varchar,qty_minim) FROM deleted),
'm_barang_divisi_diskon__delete'

INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT(
    SELECT('DELETE FROM m_barang_divisi_diskon WHERE kd_divisi='''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''' AND kd_barang = '''+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+''' AND kd_satuan='''+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+''' AND qty_minim='''+convert(varchar,qty_minim)+''';') FROM deleted)
,dbo.getDivisionID()
,(SELECT 'key__kd_barang__'+convert(varchar,kd_barang)+';key__kd_satuan__'+convert(varchar,kd_satuan)+';key__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';key__qty_minim__'+convert(varchar,qty_minim) FROM deleted),
'm_barang_divisi_diskon__delete'


FROM deleted 
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end


----- delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm on t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm WHERE kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm), 't_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm WHERE kd_pengisian_bbm='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__kd_pengisian_bbm__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pengisian_bbm) ,'t_kendaraan_pengisian_bbm'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end


----- insert_temp_m_t_penjualan -----
CREATE TRIGGER insert_temp_m_t_penjualan on t_penjualan
AFTER INSERT
as
begin
DECLARE @kd_div char(6),@primary varchar(40)
SELECT @primary = no_transaksi
FROM inserted
set @kd_div = (SELECT TOP 1
    divisi_id as divisi
    FROM tbl_log
    WHERE aksi LIKE '%t_penjualan%' AND aksi LIKE '%'+@primary+'%' AND aksi LIKE '%INSERT%' AND waktu < GETDATE() and waktu >= DATEADD(minute,-5,getdate()))
if (@kd_div is null)
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_penjualan (no_transaksi,kd_customer,kd_divisi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,kd_voucher,no_bukti,tanggal,tanggal_jatuh_tempo,status,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,diskon_uang,pajak,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server,tanggal_setor,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_kas)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_voucher)+''','''+convert(varchar,no_bukti)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal),108)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_jatuh_tempo),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon_uang)+''','''+convert(varchar,pajak)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_user)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_server),108)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_setor),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+';val__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar,kd_kas)+';val__kd_voucher__'+convert(varchar,kd_voucher)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar,no_bukti)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal),108)+';val__tanggal_jatuh_tempo__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_jatuh_tempo),108)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__diskon_uang__'+convert(varchar,diskon_uang)+';val__pajak__'+convert(varchar,pajak)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar,kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_server),108)+';val__tanggal_setor__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_setor),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
't_penjualan__insert'
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT
(SELECT 'INSERT IGNORE INTO t_penjualan (no_transaksi,kd_customer,kd_divisi,kd_jenis,kd_kas,kd_voucher,no_bukti,tanggal,tanggal_jatuh_tempo,status,diskon1,diskon2,diskon3,diskon4,diskon_uang,pajak,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server,tanggal_setor,divisi_id) VALUES('''+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_jenis)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_kas)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_voucher)+''','''+convert(varchar,no_bukti)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal),108)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_jatuh_tempo),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,status)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon1)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon2)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon3)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon4)+''','''+convert(varchar,diskon_uang)+''','''+convert(varchar,pajak)+''','''+convert(varchar,keterangan)+''','''+convert(varchar,kd_user)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_server),108)+''','''+cast(YEAR(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_setor),108)+''','''+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID())+''');' FROM inserted)
,dbo.getDivisionID(),
(SELECT 'val__no_transaksi__'+convert(varchar,no_transaksi)+';val__kd_customer__'+convert(varchar,kd_customer COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1_CI_AS)+';val__kd_divisi__'+convert(varchar,kd_divisi)+';val__kd_jenis__'+convert(varchar,kd_jenis)+';val__kd_kas__'+convert(varchar,kd_kas)+';val__kd_voucher__'+convert(varchar,kd_voucher)+';val__no_bukti__'+convert(varchar,no_bukti)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal),108)+';val__tanggal_jatuh_tempo__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_jatuh_tempo) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_jatuh_tempo),108)+';val__status__'+convert(varchar,status)+';val__diskon1__'+convert(varchar,diskon1)+';val__diskon2__'+convert(varchar,diskon2)+';val__diskon3__'+convert(varchar,diskon3)+';val__diskon4__'+convert(varchar,diskon4)+';val__diskon_uang__'+convert(varchar,diskon_uang)+';val__pajak__'+convert(varchar,pajak)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar,keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar,kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_server),108)+';val__tanggal_setor__'+cast(YEAR(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(tanggal_setor) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,tanggal_setor),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()) FROM inserted),
't_penjualan__insert'
end
else
begin
select ''
end

end

AFTER UPDATE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_kendaraan_perawatan set no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+''',kd_jenis_perawatan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+''',tanggal='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',KM='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+''' AND kd_jenis_perawatan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+''' AND tanggal='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+';key__kd_jenis_perawatan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+';key__tanggal__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal)+ ';val__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+';val__kd_jenis_perawatan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__KM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_perawatan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_STNK,kd_jenis_perawatan,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_STNK,kd_jenis_perawatan,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON inserted_data.key_no=deleted_data.key_no
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'UPDATE t_kendaraan_perawatan set no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+''',kd_jenis_perawatan='''+ CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+''',KM='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+''',keterangan='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+''',kd_user='''+ convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+''',tanggal_server='''+ cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+'''' +' WHERE no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+''' AND kd_jenis_perawatan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+''' AND tanggal='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal)+''';', dbo.getDivisionID(), 'key__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+';key__kd_jenis_perawatan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+';key__tanggal__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal)+ ';val__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.no_STNK)+';val__kd_jenis_perawatan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+';val__tanggal__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal),108)+';val__KM__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.KM)+';val__keterangan__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.keterangan)+';val__kd_user__'+convert(varchar(255),inserted_data.kd_user)+';val__tanggal_server__'+cast(YEAR(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(4)) + '-' + CAST(MONTH(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2)) + '-' + CAST(DAY(inserted_data.tanggal_server) AS VARCHAR(2))+' '+convert(char(8),convert(datetime,inserted_data.tanggal_server),108)+';val__divisi_id__'+convert(varchar,dbo.getDivisionID()), 't_kendaraan_perawatan__update' FROM (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM inserted)) AS key_no,no_STNK,kd_jenis_perawatan,tanggal,KM,keterangan,kd_user,tanggal_server FROM inserted) inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT ----- delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_perawatan -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_perawatan on t_kendaraan_perawatan
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_perawatan WHERE no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+''' AND kd_jenis_perawatan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+''' AND tanggal='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+';key__kd_jenis_perawatan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+';key__tanggal__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal), 't_kendaraan_perawatan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_perawatan WHERE no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+''' AND kd_jenis_perawatan='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+''' AND tanggal='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+';key__kd_jenis_perawatan__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_jenis_perawatan)+';key__tanggal__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.tanggal) ,'t_kendaraan_perawatan'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end

inserted_data INNER JOIN (SELECT ROW_NUMBER() OVER(ORDER BY (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM deleted)) AS key_no,no_STNK,kd_pegawai,tanggal,keterangan,kd_user,Tanggal_server FROM deleted) deleted_data ON ----- delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab -----
CREATE TRIGGER delete_temp_m_t_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab on t_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab
AFTER DELETE
as
begin
INSERT INTO tbl_tmp_post(query,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab WHERE no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai), 't_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
INSERT INTO tbl_log_transaksi(aksi,divisi_id,formatted_data,table_aksi) SELECT 'DELETE FROM t_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab WHERE no_STNK='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+''' AND kd_pegawai='''+ convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai)+''';' ,dbo.getDivisionID() ,'key__no_STNK__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.no_STNK)+';key__kd_pegawai__'+convert(varchar(255),deleted_data.kd_pegawai) ,'t_kendaraan_tanggung_jawab'__delete FROM deleted deleted_data
end
